LLM RESPONSE:
Below is one complete solution. In our answer the only “optimized‐for‐latency” change was to pipeline the for‑loop inside sampler_hls (via the HLS pragma). In addition, to resolve the linker errors (caused by the fact that the “no_structs” functions were defined as static in their own source file) we “promote” them to have external linkage by removing the static keyword. (In an actual design the child functions would be compiled into the same HLS project so that their definitions are available; here we include them so that the linker finds them.) Note that none of the mathematical logic is changed. 

Below is the complete code (which you can break up into separate files as needed):

------------------------------------------------
  
```c
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

/* 
 * NOTE: The floating‐point “fpr” type and helper functions
 * (fpr_floor, fpr_sub, fpr_of, fpr_half, fpr_sqr, fpr_mul, fpr_inv_2sqrsigma0, etc.)
 * are assumed provided elsewhere.
 */
 
typedef uint64_t fpr;
 
// Declarations for the child functions (with external linkage)
uint64_t prng_get_u64_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);
void prng_refill_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);
unsigned prng_get_u8_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);
int gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);
int BerExp_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256], fpr x, fpr ccs);
 
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Optimized sampler_hls (targeting low latency):
  The only change from the original version is the HLS pipeline 
  directive inside the for-loop.
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
int sampler_hls(fpr mu, fpr isigma, uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], 
                uint8_t p_state[256], fpr sigma_min)
{
    int s;
    fpr r;
    fpr dss;
    fpr ccs;
    int z0, z;
    int b;
    fpr x;

    s = (int)fpr_floor(mu);
    r = fpr_sub(mu, fpr_of(s));
    dss = fpr_half(fpr_sqr(isigma));
    ccs = fpr_mul(isigma, sigma_min);
    for (;;)
    {
        #pragma hls_pipeline_init_interval 1
        z0 = gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
        b = ((int)prng_get_u8_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state)) & 1;
        z = b + (((b << 1) - 1) * z0);
        x = fpr_mul(fpr_sqr(fpr_sub(fpr_of(z), r)), dss);
        x = fpr_sub(x, fpr_mul(fpr_of(z0 * z0), fpr_inv_2sqrsigma0));
        if (BerExp_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state, x, ccs))
        {
            return s + z;
        }
    }
}
 
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Below are the definitions for the “no_structs” child functions. 
  (They are copied from the original source but with the “static” removed
  so that they have external linkage and the linker finds them.)
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
 
uint64_t prng_get_u64_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    size_t u = p_ptr[0];
    if (u >= (512 - 9))
    {
        prng_refill_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
        u = 0;
    }
    p_ptr[0] = u + 8;
    return ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 0]) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 1] << 8) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 2] << 16) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 3] << 24) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 4] << 32) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 5] << 40) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 6] << 48) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 7] << 56);
}
 
unsigned prng_get_u8_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    unsigned v = p_buf[p_ptr[0]];
    p_ptr[0]++;
    if (p_ptr[0] == 512)
    {
        prng_refill_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    }
    return v;
}
 
int gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    static const uint32_t dist[] = {
        10745844u, 3068844u, 3741698u,
        5559083u, 1580863u, 8248194u,
        2260429u, 13669192u, 2736639u,
        708981u, 4421575u, 10046180u,
        169348u, 7122675u, 4136815u,
        30538u, 13063405u, 7650655u,
        4132u, 14505003u, 7826148u,
        417u, 16768101u, 11363290u,
        31u, 8444042u, 8086568u,
        1u, 12844466u, 265321u,
        0u, 1232676u, 13644283u,
        0u, 38047u, 9111839u,
        0u, 870u, 6138264u,
        0u, 14u, 12545723u,
        0u, 0u, 3104126u,
        0u, 0u, 28824u,
        0u, 0u, 198u,
        0u, 0u, 1u};
    
    uint32_t v0, v1, v2, hi;
    uint64_t lo;
    size_t u;
    int z;

    lo = prng_get_u64_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    hi = prng_get_u8_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    v0 = (uint32_t)lo & 0xFFFFFF;
    v1 = (uint32_t)(lo >> 24) & 0xFFFFFF;
    v2 = (uint32_t)(lo >> 48) | ((uint32_t)hi << 16);

    z = 0;
    for (u = 0; u < (sizeof(dist) / sizeof(dist[0])); u += 3)
    {
        uint32_t w0, w1, w2, cc;
        w0 = dist[u + 2];
        w1 = dist[u + 1];
        w2 = dist[u + 0];
        cc = (v0 - w0) >> 31;
        cc = (v1 - w1 - cc) >> 31;
        cc = (v2 - w2 - cc) >> 31;
        z += (int)cc;
    }
    return z;
}
 
void prng_refill_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    static const uint32_t CW[4] = {0x61707865, 0x3320646e, 0x79622d32, 0x6b206574};
    uint64_t cc;
    size_t u;
    
    /* Read cc from p_state offset 48 (little‐endian) */
    cc = ((uint64_t)p_state[48]) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[49] << 8) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[50] << 16) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[51] << 24) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[52] << 32) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[53] << 40) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[54] << 48) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[55] << 56);
    
    for (u = 0; u < 8; u++)
    {
        uint32_t state[16];
        size_t v;
        int i;
        for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++)
            state[j] = CW[j];
        for (int j = 0; j < 12; j++)
        {
            int idx = j * 4;
            state[j + 4] = ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 3] << 24);
        }
        state[14] ^= (uint32_t)cc;
        state[15] ^= (uint32_t)(cc >> 32);
        for (i = 0; i < 10; i++)
        {
#define QROUND(a, b, c, d)                              \
            do {                                         \
                state[a] += state[b];                    \
                state[d] ^= state[a];                    \
                state[d] = (state[d] << 16) | (state[d] >> 16);  \
                state[c] += state[d];                    \
                state[b] ^= state[c];                    \
                state[b] = (state[b] << 12) | (state[b] >> 20);  \
                state[a] += state[b];                    \
                state[d] ^= state[a];                    \
                state[d] = (state[d] << 8) | (state[d] >> 24);   \
                state[c] += state[d];                    \
                state[b] ^= state[c];                    \
                state[b] = (state[b] << 7) | (state[b] >> 25);   \
            } while (0)
            QROUND(0, 4, 8, 12);
            QROUND(1, 5, 9, 13);
            QROUND(2, 6, 10, 14);
            QROUND(3, 7, 11, 15);
            QROUND(0, 5, 10, 15);
            QROUND(1, 6, 11, 12);
            QROUND(2, 7, 8, 13);
            QROUND(3, 4, 9, 14);
#undef QROUND
        }
        for (v = 0; v < 4; v++)
        {
            state[v] += CW[v];
        }
        for (v = 0; v < 10; v++)
        {
            int byte_idx = v * 4;
            uint32_t tmp = ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 3] << 24);
            state[v + 4] += tmp;
        }
        {
            int byte_idx = 10 * 4;
            uint32_t tmp = ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 3] << 24);
            state[14] += tmp ^ (uint32_t)cc;
        }
        {
            int byte_idx = 11 * 4;
            uint32_t tmp = ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 3] << 24);
            state[15] += tmp ^ (uint32_t)(cc >> 32);
        }
        cc++;
        for (v = 0; v < 16; v++)
        {
            int base = (u << 2) + (v << 5);
            p_buf[base + 0] = (uint8_t)state[v];
            p_buf[base + 1] = (uint8_t)(state[v] >> 8);
            p_buf[base + 2] = (uint8_t)(state[v] >> 16);
            p_buf[base + 3] = (uint8_t)(state[v] >> 24);
        }
    }
    /* Write updated cc back into p_state at offset 48 (little‐endian) */
    p_state[48] = (uint8_t)cc;
    p_state[49] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 8);
    p_state[50] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 16);
    p_state[51] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 24);
    p_state[52] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 32);
    p_state[53] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 40);
    p_state[54] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 48);
    p_state[55] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 56);
 
    p_ptr[0] = 0;
}
 
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  The sampler() function is not modified.
  (Its definition is assumed to be provided elsewhere.)
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
 
// End of file
```

    #pragma hls_pipeline_init_interval 1

which forces an initiation interval of 1 cycle (thus reducing latency).
Furthermore, the child “no_structs” functions are provided here with external linkage so that the linker finds them.

Below is an updated complete code example. We add an extern declaration for sampler so that its symbol is known (without changing its definition, which is assumed provided elsewhere). This resolves the “undeclared identifier ‘sampler’” error while leaving sampler’s logic intact.

```c
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

/* Assume the following floating‐point functions and constants (like fpr_floor,
   fpr_sub, fpr_of, fpr_half, fpr_sqr, fpr_mul, fpr_inv_2sqrsigma0, etc.) are
   defined elsewhere. We only provide their declarations here. */
typedef uint64_t fpr;
extern fpr fpr_floor(fpr x);
extern fpr fpr_sub(fpr x, fpr y);
extern fpr fpr_of(int64_t i);
extern fpr fpr_half(fpr x);
extern fpr fpr_sqr(fpr x);
extern fpr fpr_mul(fpr x, fpr y);
extern const fpr fpr_inv_2sqrsigma0;

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Extern declarations for child “no_structs” functions with external linkage.
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
extern uint64_t prng_get_u64_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);
extern void prng_refill_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);
extern unsigned prng_get_u8_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);
extern int gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);
extern int BerExp_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256], fpr x, fpr ccs);

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Extern declaration for sampler (do not modify its implementation).
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
extern int sampler(void *ctx, fpr mu, fpr isigma);

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Optimized sampler_hls (targeting low latency).
  This function is modified with a pipeline directive and remains functionally equivalent.
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
int sampler_hls(fpr mu, fpr isigma, uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1],
                uint8_t p_state[256], fpr sigma_min)
{
    int s;
    fpr r;
    fpr dss;
    fpr ccs;
    int z0, z;
    int b;
    fpr x;

    s = (int)fpr_floor(mu);
    r = fpr_sub(mu, fpr_of(s));
    dss = fpr_half(fpr_sqr(isigma));
    ccs = fpr_mul(isigma, sigma_min);
    for (;;)
    {
        #pragma hls_pipeline_init_interval 1
        z0 = gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
        b = ((int)prng_get_u8_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state)) & 1;
        z = b + (((b << 1) - 1) * z0);
        x = fpr_mul(fpr_sqr(fpr_sub(fpr_of(z), r)), dss);
        x = fpr_sub(x, fpr_mul(fpr_of(z0 * z0), fpr_inv_2sqrsigma0));
        if (BerExp_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state, x, ccs))
        {
            return s + z;
        }
    }
}

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Below are definitions for the “no_structs” child functions, now provided
  with external linkage (i.e. without the "static" specifier).
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
uint64_t prng_get_u64_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    size_t u = p_ptr[0];
    if (u >= (512 - 9))
    {
        prng_refill_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
        u = 0;
    }
    p_ptr[0] = u + 8;
    return ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 0]) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 1] << 8) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 2] << 16) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 3] << 24) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 4] << 32) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 5] << 40) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 6] << 48) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 7] << 56);
}

unsigned prng_get_u8_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    unsigned v = p_buf[p_ptr[0]];
    p_ptr[0]++;
    if (p_ptr[0] == 512)
    {
        prng_refill_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    }
    return v;
}

int gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    static const uint32_t dist[] = {
         10745844u, 3068844u, 3741698u,
         5559083u, 1580863u, 8248194u,
         2260429u, 13669192u, 2736639u,
         708981u, 4421575u, 10046180u,
         169348u, 7122675u, 4136815u,
         30538u, 13063405u, 7650655u,
         4132u, 14505003u, 7826148u,
         417u, 16768101u, 11363290u,
         31u, 8444042u, 8086568u,
         1u, 12844466u, 265321u,
         0u, 1232676u, 13644283u,
         0u, 38047u, 9111839u,
         0u, 870u, 6138264u,
         0u, 14u, 12545723u,
         0u, 0u, 3104126u,
         0u, 0u, 28824u,
         0u, 0u, 198u,
         0u, 0u, 1u};

    uint32_t v0, v1, v2, hi;
    uint64_t lo;
    size_t u;
    int z;

    lo = prng_get_u64_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    hi = prng_get_u8_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    v0 = (uint32_t)lo & 0xFFFFFF;
    v1 = (uint32_t)(lo >> 24) & 0xFFFFFF;
    v2 = (uint32_t)(lo >> 48) | ((uint32_t)hi << 16);

    z = 0;
    for (u = 0; u < (sizeof(dist) / sizeof(dist[0])); u += 3)
    {
        uint32_t w0, w1, w2, cc;
        w0 = dist[u + 2];
        w1 = dist[u + 1];
        w2 = dist[u + 0];
        cc = (v0 - w0) >> 31;
        cc = (v1 - w1 - cc) >> 31;
        cc = (v2 - w2 - cc) >> 31;
        z += (int)cc;
    }
    return z;
}

void prng_refill_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    static const uint32_t CW[4] = {0x61707865, 0x3320646e, 0x79622d32, 0x6b206574};
    uint64_t cc;
    size_t u;
    
    /* Read cc from p_state offset 48 (little-endian) */
    cc = ((uint64_t)p_state[48]) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[49] << 8) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[50] << 16) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[51] << 24) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[52] << 32) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[53] << 40) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[54] << 48) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[55] << 56);
    
    for (u = 0; u < 8; u++)
    {
        uint32_t state[16];
        size_t v;
        int i;
        for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++)
            state[j] = CW[j];
        for (int j = 0; j < 12; j++)
        {
            int idx = j * 4;
            state[j + 4] = ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 3] << 24);
        }
        state[14] ^= (uint32_t)cc;
        state[15] ^= (uint32_t)(cc >> 32);
        for (i = 0; i < 10; i++)
        {
#define QROUND(a, b, c, d)                              \
            do {                                         \
                state[a] += state[b];                    \
                state[d] ^= state[a];                    \
                state[d] = (state[d] << 16) | (state[d] >> 16);  \
                state[c] += state[d];                    \
                state[b] ^= state[c];                    \
                state[b] = (state[b] << 12) | (state[b] >> 20);  \
                state[a] += state[b];                    \
                state[d] ^= state[a];                    \
                state[d] = (state[d] << 8) | (state[d] >> 24);   \
                state[c] += state[d];                    \
                state[b] ^= state[c];                    \
                state[b] = (state[b] << 7) | (state[b] >> 25);   \
            } while (0)
            QROUND(0, 4, 8, 12);
            QROUND(1, 5, 9, 13);
            QROUND(2, 6, 10, 14);
            QROUND(3, 7, 11, 15);
            QROUND(0, 5, 10, 15);
            QROUND(1, 6, 11, 12);
            QROUND(2, 7, 8, 13);
            QROUND(3, 4, 9, 14);
#undef QROUND
        }
        for (v = 0; v < 4; v++)
        {
            state[v] += CW[v];
        }
        for (v = 0; v < 10; v++)
        {
            int byte_idx = v * 4;
            uint32_t tmp = ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 3] << 24);
            state[v + 4] += tmp;
        }
        {
            int byte_idx = 10 * 4;
            uint32_t tmp = ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 3] << 24);
            state[14] += tmp ^ (uint32_t)cc;
        }
        {
            int byte_idx = 11 * 4;
            uint32_t tmp = ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 3] << 24);
            state[15] += tmp ^ (uint32_t)(cc >> 32);
        }
        cc++;
        for (v = 0; v < 16; v++)
        {
            int base = (u << 2) + (v << 5);
            p_buf[base + 0] = (uint8_t)state[v];
            p_buf[base + 1] = (uint8_t)(state[v] >> 8);
            p_buf[base + 2] = (uint8_t)(state[v] >> 16);
            p_buf[base + 3] = (uint8_t)(state[v] >> 24);
        }
    }
    /* Write updated cc back to p_state at offset 48 (little-endian) */
    p_state[48] = (uint8_t)cc;
    p_state[49] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 8);
    p_state[50] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 16);
    p_state[51] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 24);
    p_state[52] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 32);
    p_state[53] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 40);
    p_state[54] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 48);
    p_state[55] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 56);
 
    p_ptr[0] = 0;
}
```

LLM RESPONSE:
Below is a revised complete code example. In this version we remove the conflicting prototype declarations for fpr_floor (and related fpr_* functions) so that they’ll be assumed to be provided elsewhere (for example, via an included header). This avoids the “functions that differ only in their return type cannot be overloaded” error. The rest of the code is unchanged, and the sampler_hls function remains optimized with the HLS pipeline directive.

------------------------------------------------

```c
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

/*
 * NOTE:
 * The following floating‐point functions and constant (fpr_floor, fpr_sub, 
 * fpr_of, fpr_half, fpr_sqr, fpr_mul, fpr_inv_2sqrsigma0, etc.) are assumed to 
 * be defined/prototyped elsewhere. Hence, we remove any conflicting prototypes.
 *
 * For example, you might include a header file here:
 *
 *    #include "fpr.h"
 *
 * and it would provide the proper declarations.
 */

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Extern declarations for child “no_structs” functions with external linkage.
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
extern uint64_t prng_get_u64_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);
extern void prng_refill_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);
extern unsigned prng_get_u8_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);
extern int gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);
extern int BerExp_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256], uint64_t x, uint64_t ccs);

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Extern declaration for sampler (do not modify its implementation).
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
extern int sampler(void *ctx, uint64_t mu, uint64_t isigma);

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Optimized sampler_hls (targeting low latency).
  This function is modified with a pipeline directive and remains functionally equivalent.
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
int sampler_hls(uint64_t mu, uint64_t isigma,
                uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1],
                uint8_t p_state[256], uint64_t sigma_min)
{
    int s;
    uint64_t r;
    uint64_t dss;
    uint64_t ccs;
    int z0, z;
    int b;
    uint64_t x;

    /* 
     * These operations assume that fpr_floor, fpr_sub, fpr_of, fpr_half, 
     * fpr_sqr, and fpr_mul operate on type uint64_t and are defined elsewhere.
     */
    s = (int)fpr_floor(mu);
    r = fpr_sub(mu, fpr_of(s));
    dss = fpr_half(fpr_sqr(isigma));
    ccs = fpr_mul(isigma, sigma_min);
    for (;;)
    {
        #pragma hls_pipeline_init_interval 1
        z0 = gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
        b  = ((int)prng_get_u8_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state)) & 1;
        z  = b + (((b << 1) - 1) * z0);
        x  = fpr_mul(fpr_sqr(fpr_sub(fpr_of(z), r)), dss);
        x  = fpr_sub(x, fpr_mul(fpr_of(z0 * z0), fpr_inv_2sqrsigma0));
        if (BerExp_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state, x, ccs))
        {
            return s + z;
        }
    }
}

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Below are definitions for the “no_structs” child functions, provided
  with external linkage (i.e. without the "static" specifier).
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
uint64_t prng_get_u64_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    size_t u = p_ptr[0];
    if (u >= (512 - 9))
    {
        prng_refill_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
        u = 0;
    }
    p_ptr[0] = u + 8;
    return ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 0]) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 1] << 8) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 2] << 16) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 3] << 24) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 4] << 32) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 5] << 40) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 6] << 48) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 7] << 56);
}

unsigned prng_get_u8_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    unsigned v = p_buf[p_ptr[0]];
    p_ptr[0]++;
    if (p_ptr[0] == 512)
    {
        prng_refill_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    }
    return v;
}

int gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    static const uint32_t dist[] = {
         10745844u, 3068844u, 3741698u,
         5559083u, 1580863u, 8248194u,
         2260429u, 13669192u, 2736639u,
         708981u, 4421575u, 10046180u,
         169348u, 7122675u, 4136815u,
         30538u, 13063405u, 7650655u,
         4132u, 14505003u, 7826148u,
         417u, 16768101u, 11363290u,
         31u, 8444042u, 8086568u,
         1u, 12844466u, 265321u,
         0u, 1232676u, 13644283u,
         0u, 38047u, 9111839u,
         0u, 870u, 6138264u,
         0u, 14u, 12545723u,
         0u, 0u, 3104126u,
         0u, 0u, 28824u,
         0u, 0u, 198u,
         0u, 0u, 1u};

    uint32_t v0, v1, v2, hi;
    uint64_t lo;
    size_t u;
    int z;

    lo = prng_get_u64_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    hi = prng_get_u8_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    v0 = (uint32_t)lo & 0xFFFFFF;
    v1 = (uint32_t)(lo >> 24) & 0xFFFFFF;
    v2 = (uint32_t)(lo >> 48) | ((uint32_t)hi << 16);

    z = 0;
    for (u = 0; u < (sizeof(dist) / sizeof(dist[0])); u += 3)
    {
        uint32_t w0, w1, w2, cc;
        w0 = dist[u + 2];
        w1 = dist[u + 1];
        w2 = dist[u + 0];
        cc = (v0 - w0) >> 31;
        cc = (v1 - w1 - cc) >> 31;
        cc = (v2 - w2 - cc) >> 31;
        z += (int)cc;
    }
    return z;
}

void prng_refill_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    static const uint32_t CW[4] = {0x61707865, 0x3320646e, 0x79622d32, 0x6b206574};
    uint64_t cc;
    size_t u;
    
    cc = ((uint64_t)p_state[48]) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[49] << 8) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[50] << 16) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[51] << 24) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[52] << 32) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[53] << 40) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[54] << 48) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[55] << 56);
    
    for (u = 0; u < 8; u++)
    {
        uint32_t state[16];
        size_t v;
        int i;
        for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++)
            state[j] = CW[j];
        for (int j = 0; j < 12; j++)
        {
            int idx = j * 4;
            state[j + 4] = ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 3] << 24);
        }
        state[14] ^= (uint32_t)cc;
        state[15] ^= (uint32_t)(cc >> 32);
        for (i = 0; i < 10; i++)
        {
#define QROUND(a, b, c, d)                              \
            do {                                         \
                state[a] += state[b];                    \
                state[d] ^= state[a];                    \
                state[d] = (state[d] << 16) | (state[d] >> 16);  \
                state[c] += state[d];                    \
                state[b] ^= state[c];                    \
                state[b] = (state[b] << 12) | (state[b] >> 20);  \
                state[a] += state[b];                    \
                state[d] ^= state[a];                    \
                state[d] = (state[d] << 8) | (state[d] >> 24);   \
                state[c] += state[d];                    \
                state[b] ^= state[c];                    \
                state[b] = (state[b] << 7) | (state[b] >> 25);   \
            } while (0)
            QROUND(0, 4, 8, 12);
            QROUND(1, 5, 9, 13);
            QROUND(2, 6, 10, 14);
            QROUND(3, 7, 11, 15);
            QROUND(0, 5, 10, 15);
            QROUND(1, 6, 11, 12);
            QROUND(2, 7, 8, 13);
            QROUND(3, 4, 9, 14);
#undef QROUND
        }
        for (v = 0; v < 4; v++)
        {
            state[v] += CW[v];
        }
        for (v = 0; v < 10; v++)
        {
            int byte_idx = v * 4;
            uint32_t tmp = ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 3] << 24);
            state[v + 4] += tmp;
        }
        {
            int byte_idx = 10 * 4;
            uint32_t tmp = ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 3] << 24);
            state[14] += tmp ^ (uint32_t)cc;
        }
        {
            int byte_idx = 11 * 4;
            uint32_t tmp = ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 3] << 24);
            state[15] += tmp ^ (uint32_t)(cc >> 32);
        }
        cc++;
        for (v = 0; v < 16; v++)
        {
            int base = (u << 2) + (v << 5);
            p_buf[base + 0] = (uint8_t)state[v];
            p_buf[base + 1] = (uint8_t)(state[v] >> 8);
            p_buf[base + 2] = (uint8_t)(state[v] >> 16);
            p_buf[base + 3] = (uint8_t)(state[v] >> 24);
        }
    }
    p_state[48] = (uint8_t)cc;
    p_state[49] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 8);
    p_state[50] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 16);
    p_state[51] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 24);
    p_state[52] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 32);
    p_state[53] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 40);
    p_state[54] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 48);
    p_state[55] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 56);
 
    p_ptr[0] = 0;
}

/*
 * The sampler() function is assumed to be defined elsewhere.
 * Its definition is not modified.
 */
```

Below is a revised complete code example. In this version we now supply (with external linkage) a full definition for BerExp_no_structs so that linking succeeds. (All floating‐point helper functions and constants are assumed provided elsewhere.) Note that we treat our custom floating‐point type fpr as uint64_t, as in previous versions.

```c
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

/*
 * Assume that fpr and its helper functions/constants are defined externally.
 * For instance, one might have a header "fpr.h" that provides:
 *
 *    typedef uint64_t fpr;
 *    fpr fpr_floor(fpr x);
 *    fpr fpr_sub(fpr x, fpr y);
 *    fpr fpr_of(int64_t i);
 *    fpr fpr_half(fpr x);
 *    fpr fpr_sqr(fpr x);
 *    fpr fpr_mul(fpr x, fpr y);
 *    fpr fpr_trunc(fpr x);
 *    fpr fpr_expm_p63(fpr x, fpr ccs);
 *    extern const fpr fpr_inv_2sqrsigma0;
 *    extern const fpr fpr_inv_log2;
 *    extern const fpr fpr_log2;
 *
 * For this example, we assume the above exist.
 */

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Extern declarations for child “no_structs” functions with external linkage.
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
extern uint64_t prng_get_u64_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);
extern void prng_refill_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);
extern unsigned prng_get_u8_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);
extern int gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  The BerExp_no_structs function is defined below (its original version
  had static linkage; here we remove "static" for external linkage so that
  the linker finds it).
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
int BerExp_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256], uint64_t x, uint64_t ccs)
{
    int s, i;
    uint64_t r;
    uint32_t sw, w;
    uint64_t z;

    /* 
     * Compute s = floor( x * fpr_inv_log2 ). fpr_trunc and fpr_mul
     * are assumed to work on fpr (uint64_t) as defined externally.
     */
    s = (int)fpr_trunc(fpr_mul(x, fpr_inv_log2));
    r = fpr_sub(x, fpr_mul(fpr_of(s), fpr_log2));
    sw = (uint32_t)s;
    sw ^= (sw ^ 63U) & -((63U - sw) >> 31);
    s = (int)sw;
    z = ((fpr_expm_p63(r, ccs) << 1) - 1) >> s;
    i = 64;
    do
    {
        i -= 8;
        w = prng_get_u8_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state) - (((uint32_t)(z >> i)) & 0xFF);
    } while (!w && i > 0);
    return (int)(w >> 31);
}

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Extern declaration for sampler (its definition is assumed to be provided elsewhere).
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
extern int sampler(void *ctx, uint64_t mu, uint64_t isigma);

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Optimized sampler_hls (targeting low latency).
  A pipeline directive reduces latency; no logic is modified.
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
int sampler_hls(uint64_t mu, uint64_t isigma,
                uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1],
                uint8_t p_state[256], uint64_t sigma_min)
{
    int s;
    uint64_t r;
    uint64_t dss;
    uint64_t ccs;
    int z0, z;
    int b;
    uint64_t x;

    s = (int)fpr_floor(mu);
    r = fpr_sub(mu, fpr_of(s));
    dss = fpr_half(fpr_sqr(isigma));
    ccs = fpr_mul(isigma, sigma_min);
    for (;;)
    {
        #pragma hls_pipeline_init_interval 1
        z0 = gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
        b  = ((int)prng_get_u8_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state)) & 1;
        z  = b + (((b << 1) - 1) * z0);
        x  = fpr_mul(fpr_sqr(fpr_sub(fpr_of(z), r)), dss);
        x  = fpr_sub(x, fpr_mul(fpr_of(z0 * z0), fpr_inv_2sqrsigma0));
        if (BerExp_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state, x, ccs))
        {
            return s + z;
        }
    }
}

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Definitions for the “no_structs” child functions (provided with external linkage).
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
uint64_t prng_get_u64_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    size_t u = p_ptr[0];
    if (u >= (512 - 9))
    {
        prng_refill_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
        u = 0;
    }
    p_ptr[0] = u + 8;
    return ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 0]) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 1] << 8) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 2] << 16) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 3] << 24) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 4] << 32) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 5] << 40) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 6] << 48) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 7] << 56);
}

unsigned prng_get_u8_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    unsigned v = p_buf[p_ptr[0]];
    p_ptr[0]++;
    if (p_ptr[0] == 512)
    {
        prng_refill_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    }
    return v;
}

int gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    static const uint32_t dist[] = {
         10745844u, 3068844u, 3741698u,
         5559083u, 1580863u, 8248194u,
         2260429u, 13669192u, 2736639u,
         708981u, 4421575u, 10046180u,
         169348u, 7122675u, 4136815u,
         30538u, 13063405u, 7650655u,
         4132u, 14505003u, 7826148u,
         417u, 16768101u, 11363290u,
         31u, 8444042u, 8086568u,
         1u, 12844466u, 265321u,
         0u, 1232676u, 13644283u,
         0u, 38047u, 9111839u,
         0u, 870u, 6138264u,
         0u, 14u, 12545723u,
         0u, 0u, 3104126u,
         0u, 0u, 28824u,
         0u, 0u, 198u,
         0u, 0u, 1u};

    uint32_t v0, v1, v2, hi;
    uint64_t lo;
    size_t u;
    int z;

    lo = prng_get_u64_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    hi = prng_get_u8_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    v0 = (uint32_t)lo & 0xFFFFFF;
    v1 = (uint32_t)(lo >> 24) & 0xFFFFFF;
    v2 = (uint32_t)(lo >> 48) | ((uint32_t)hi << 16);

    z = 0;
    for (u = 0; u < (sizeof(dist) / sizeof(dist[0])); u += 3)
    {
        uint32_t w0, w1, w2, cc;
        w0 = dist[u + 2];
        w1 = dist[u + 1];
        w2 = dist[u + 0];
        cc = (v0 - w0) >> 31;
        cc = (v1 - w1 - cc) >> 31;
        cc = (v2 - w2 - cc) >> 31;
        z += (int)cc;
    }
    return z;
}

void prng_refill_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    static const uint32_t CW[4] = {0x61707865, 0x3320646e, 0x79622d32, 0x6b206574};
    uint64_t cc;
    size_t u;
    
    cc = ((uint64_t)p_state[48]) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[49] << 8) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[50] << 16) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[51] << 24) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[52] << 32) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[53] << 40) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[54] << 48) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[55] << 56);

        uint32_t state[16];

        int i;

            state[j] = CW[j];
        for (int j = 0; j < 12; j++)
        {
            int idx = j * 4;
            state[j + 4] = ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 3] << 24);
        }
        state[14] ^= (uint32_t)cc;
        state[15] ^= (uint32_t)(cc >> 32);
        for (i = 0; i < 10; i++)
        {
#define QROUND(a, b, c, d)                              \
            do {                                         \
                state[a] += state[b];                    \
                state[d] ^= state[a];                    \
                state[d] = (state[d] << 16) | (state[d] >> 16);  \
                state[c] += state[d];                    \
                state[b] ^= state[c];                    \
                state[b] = (state[b] << 12) | (state[b] >> 20);  \
                state[a] += state[b];                    \
                state[d] ^= state[a];                    \
                state[d] = (state[d] << 8) | (state[d] >> 24);   \
                state[c] += state[d];                    \
                state[b] ^= state[c];                    \
                state[b] = (state[b] << 7) | (state[b] >> 25);   \
            } while (0)
            QROUND(0, 4, 8, 12);
            QROUND(1, 5, 9, 13);
            QROUND(2, 6, 10, 14);
            QROUND(3, 7, 11, 15);
            QROUND(0, 5, 10, 15);
            QROUND(1, 6, 11, 12);
            QROUND(2, 7, 8, 13);
            QROUND(3, 4, 9, 14);
#undef QROUND
        }
        for (v = 0; v < 4; v++)
        {
            state[v] += CW[v];
        }
        for (v = 0; v < 10; v++)
        {
            int byte_idx = v * 4;
            uint32_t tmp = ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 3] << 24);
            state[v + 4] += tmp;
        }
        {
            int byte_idx = 10 * 4;
            uint32_t tmp = ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 3] << 24);
            state[14] += tmp ^ (uint32_t)cc;
        }
        {
            int byte_idx = 11 * 4;
            uint32_t tmp = ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 3] << 24);
            state[15] += tmp ^ (uint32_t)(cc >> 32);
        }
        cc++;
        for (v = 0; v < 16; v++)
        {
            int base = (u << 2) + (v << 5);
            p_buf[base + 0] = (uint8_t)state[v];
            p_buf[base + 1] = (uint8_t)(state[v] >> 8);
            p_buf[base + 2] = (uint8_t)(state[v] >> 16);
            p_buf[base + 3] = (uint8_t)(state[v] >> 24);

    p_state[48] = (uint8_t)cc;
    p_state[49] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 8);
    p_state[50] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 16);
    p_state[51] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 24);
    p_state[52] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 32);
    p_state[53] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 40);
    p_state[54] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 48);
    p_state[55] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 56);
 
    p_ptr[0] = 0;
}
```

```c
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  To resolve linkage we now provide a full definition for BerExp_no_structs.
  (Its logic is unchanged from the original reference implementation.)
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
int BerExp_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256], fpr x, fpr ccs)
{
    int s, i;
    fpr r;
    uint32_t sw, w;
    fpr z;

    s = (int)fpr_trunc(fpr_mul(x, fpr_inv_log2));
    r = fpr_sub(x, fpr_mul(fpr_of(s), fpr_log2));

    } while (!w && i > 0);
    return (int)(w >> 31);
}

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  The optimized sampler_hls function (targeting low latency).
  Its only change is the pipeline directive.
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
int sampler_hls(fpr mu, fpr isigma,
                uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1],
                uint8_t p_state[256], fpr sigma_min)
{
    int s;
    fpr r;
    fpr dss;
    fpr ccs;
    int z0, z;
    int b;
    fpr x;

    s = (int)fpr_floor(mu);
    r = fpr_sub(mu, fpr_of(s));
    dss = fpr_half(fpr_sqr(isigma));
    ccs = fpr_mul(isigma, sigma_min);
    for (;;)
    {
        #pragma hls_pipeline_init_interval 1
        z0 = gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
        b  = ((int)prng_get_u8_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state)) & 1;
        z  = b + (((b << 1) - 1) * z0);
        x  = fpr_mul(fpr_sqr(fpr_sub(fpr_of(z), r)), dss);
        x  = fpr_sub(x, fpr_mul(fpr_of(z0 * z0), fpr_inv_2sqrsigma0));
        if (BerExp_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state, x, ccs))
        {
            return s + z;
        }
    }
}

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Definitions for the “no_structs” child functions.
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
uint64_t prng_get_u64_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    size_t u = p_ptr[0];
    if (u >= (512 - 9))
    {
        prng_refill_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
        u = 0;
    }
    p_ptr[0] = u + 8;
    return ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 0]) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 1] << 8) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 2] << 16) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 3] << 24) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 4] << 32) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 5] << 40) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 6] << 48) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 7] << 56);
}

unsigned prng_get_u8_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    unsigned v = p_buf[p_ptr[0]];
    p_ptr[0]++;
    if (p_ptr[0] == 512)
    {
        prng_refill_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    }
    return v;
}

int gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{

         0u, 0u, 198u,
         0u, 0u, 1u};

    uint32_t v0, v1, v2, hi;
    uint64_t lo;
    size_t u;
    int z;

    lo = prng_get_u64_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    hi = prng_get_u8_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    v0 = (uint32_t)lo & 0xFFFFFF;
    v1 = (uint32_t)(lo >> 24) & 0xFFFFFF;
    v2 = (uint32_t)(lo >> 48) | ((uint32_t)hi << 16);

    z = 0;

        cc = (v1 - w1 - cc) >> 31;
        cc = (v2 - w2 - cc) >> 31;
        z += (int)cc;
    }
    return z;

            p_buf[base + 3] = (uint8_t)(state[v] >> 24);
        }
    }
    p_state[48] = (uint8_t)cc;
    p_state[49] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 8);
    p_state[50] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 16);
    p_state[51] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 24);
    p_state[52] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 32);
    p_state[53] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 40);
    p_state[54] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 48);
    p_state[55] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 56);
 
    p_ptr[0] = 0;
}

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  The sampler function is provided as-is (do not modify) and is used for testing.
```

```c
/*

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Extern declarations for child “no_structs” functions with external linkage.

extern uint64_t prng_get_u64_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);
extern void prng_refill_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);

extern int gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256]);

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Definition for BerExp_no_structs with external linkage.
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
int BerExp_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256],
                      uint64_t x, uint64_t ccs)
{
    int s, i;
    uint64_t r;
    uint32_t sw, w;
    uint64_t z;

    s = (int)fpr_trunc(fpr_mul(x, fpr_inv_log2));
    r = fpr_sub(x, fpr_mul(fpr_of(s), fpr_log2));
    sw = (uint32_t)s;
    sw ^= (sw ^ 63U) & -((63U - sw) >> 31);

        w = prng_get_u8_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state) - (((uint32_t)(z >> i)) & 0xFF);
    } while (!w && i > 0);
    return (int)(w >> 31);
}

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Extern declaration for sampler.
  (Its definition is provided below for linking, but do not modify it if already defined in your code.)
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
extern int sampler(void *ctx, uint64_t mu, uint64_t isigma);

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Optimized sampler_hls function (targeting low latency).
  The only change is the pipeline directive.
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
int sampler_hls(uint64_t mu, uint64_t isigma,
                uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1],
                uint8_t p_state[256], uint64_t sigma_min)
{
    int s;
    uint64_t r;
    uint64_t dss;
    uint64_t ccs;
    int z0, z;
    int b;
    uint64_t x;

    s = (int)fpr_floor(mu);
    r = fpr_sub(mu, fpr_of(s));
    dss = fpr_half(fpr_sqr(isigma));
    ccs = fpr_mul(isigma, sigma_min);
    for (;;) {
        #pragma hls_pipeline_init_interval 1
        z0 = gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
        b  = ((int)prng_get_u8_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state)) & 1;
        z  = b + (((b << 1) - 1) * z0);
        x  = fpr_mul(fpr_sqr(fpr_sub(fpr_of(z), r)), dss);
        x  = fpr_sub(x, fpr_mul(fpr_of(z0 * z0), fpr_inv_2sqrsigma0));
        if (BerExp_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state, x, ccs))
            return s + z;
    }
}

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------
  Definitions for the “no_structs” child functions.
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
uint64_t prng_get_u64_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    size_t u = p_ptr[0];
    if (u >= (512 - 9)) {
        prng_refill_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
        u = 0;
    }
    p_ptr[0] = u + 8;
    return ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 0]) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 1] << 8) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 2] << 16) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 3] << 24) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 4] << 32) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 5] << 40) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 6] << 48) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 7] << 56);
}

unsigned prng_get_u8_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    unsigned v = p_buf[p_ptr[0]];
    p_ptr[0]++;
    if (p_ptr[0] == 512)
        prng_refill_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    return v;
}

int gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    static const uint32_t dist[] = {
         10745844u, 3068844u, 3741698u,
         5559083u, 1580863u, 8248194u,
         2260429u, 13669192u, 2736639u,
         708981u, 4421575u, 10046180u,
         169348u, 7122675u, 4136815u,
         30538u, 13063405u, 7650655u,
         4132u, 14505003u, 7826148u,
         417u, 16768101u, 11363290u,
         31u, 8444042u, 8086568u,
         1u, 12844466u, 265321u,
         0u, 1232676u, 13644283u,
         0u, 38047u, 9111839u,
         0u, 870u, 6138264u,
         0u, 14u, 12545723u,
         0u, 0u, 3104126u,
         0u, 0u, 28824u,
         0u, 0u, 198u,
         0u, 0u, 1u};

    uint32_t v0, v1, v2, hi;
    uint64_t lo;
    size_t u;
    int z;

    lo = prng_get_u64_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    hi = prng_get_u8_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    v0 = (uint32_t)lo & 0xFFFFFF;
    v1 = (uint32_t)(lo >> 24) & 0xFFFFFF;
    v2 = (uint32_t)(lo >> 48) | ((uint32_t)hi << 16);

    z = 0;
    for (u = 0; u < (sizeof(dist) / sizeof(dist[0])); u += 3) {
        uint32_t w0, w1, w2, cc;
        w0 = dist[u + 2];
        w1 = dist[u + 1];
        w2 = dist[u + 0];
        cc = (v0 - w0) >> 31;
        cc = (v1 - w1 - cc) >> 31;
        cc = (v2 - w2 - cc) >> 31;
        z += (int)cc;
    }
    return z;
}

void prng_refill_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    static const uint32_t CW[4] = {0x61707865, 0x3320646e, 0x79622d32, 0x6b206574};
    uint64_t cc;
    size_t u;
    
    cc = ((uint64_t)p_state[48]) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[49] << 8) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[50] << 16) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[51] << 24) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[52] << 32) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[53] << 40) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[54] << 48) |
```

```c
  Optimized sampler_hls function (targeting low latency).
  Only the pipeline directive is added.
---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
int sampler_hls(uint64_t mu, uint64_t isigma,
                uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1],
                uint8_t p_state[256], uint64_t sigma_min)
{
    int s;
    uint64_t r;
    uint64_t dss;
    uint64_t ccs;
    int z0, z;
    int b;
    uint64_t x;

    s = (int)fpr_floor(mu);
    r = fpr_sub(mu, fpr_of(s));
    dss = fpr_half(fpr_sqr(isigma));
    ccs = fpr_mul(isigma, sigma_min);
    for (;;) {

{

    if (u >= (512 - 9)) {
        prng_refill_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
        u = 0;

           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 2] << 16) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 3] << 24) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 4] << 32) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 5] << 40) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 6] << 48) |
           ((uint64_t)p_buf[u + 7] << 56);
}

unsigned prng_get_u8_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    unsigned v = p_buf[p_ptr[0]];
    p_ptr[0]++;
    if (p_ptr[0] == 512)
        prng_refill_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    return v;
}

int gaussian0_sampler_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    static const uint32_t dist[] = {
         10745844u, 3068844u, 3741698u,
         5559083u, 1580863u, 8248194u,
         2260429u, 13669192u, 2736639u,
         708981u, 4421575u, 10046180u,
         169348u, 7122675u, 4136815u,
         30538u, 13063405u, 7650655u,
         4132u, 14505003u, 7826148u,
         417u, 16768101u, 11363290u,
         31u, 8444042u, 8086568u,
         1u, 12844466u, 265321u,
         0u, 1232676u, 13644283u,
         0u, 38047u, 9111839u,
         0u, 870u, 6138264u,
         0u, 14u, 12545723u,
         0u, 0u, 3104126u,
         0u, 0u, 28824u,
         0u, 0u, 198u,
         0u, 0u, 1u};

    uint32_t v0, v1, v2, hi;
    uint64_t lo;
    size_t u;
    int z;

    lo = prng_get_u64_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    hi = prng_get_u8_no_structs(p_buf, p_ptr, p_state);
    v0 = (uint32_t)lo & 0xFFFFFF;
    v1 = (uint32_t)(lo >> 24) & 0xFFFFFF;
    v2 = (uint32_t)(lo >> 48) | ((uint32_t)hi << 16);

    z = 0;
    for (u = 0; u < (sizeof(dist) / sizeof(dist[0])); u += 3) {
        uint32_t w0, w1, w2, cc;
        w0 = dist[u + 2];
        w1 = dist[u + 1];
        w2 = dist[u + 0];
        cc = (v0 - w0) >> 31;
        cc = (v1 - w1 - cc) >> 31;
        cc = (v2 - w2 - cc) >> 31;
        z += (int)cc;
    }
    return z;
}

void prng_refill_no_structs(uint8_t p_buf[512], size_t p_ptr[1], uint8_t p_state[256])
{
    static const uint32_t CW[4] = {0x61707865, 0x3320646e, 0x79622d32, 0x6b206574};
    uint64_t cc;
    size_t u;
    
    cc = ((uint64_t)p_state[48]) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[49] << 8) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[50] << 16) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[51] << 24) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[52] << 32) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[53] << 40) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[54] << 48) |
         ((uint64_t)p_state[55] << 56);
    
    for (u = 0; u < 8; u++) {
        uint32_t state[16];
        size_t v;
        int i;
        for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++)
            state[j] = CW[j];
        for (int j = 0; j < 12; j++) {
            int idx = j * 4;
            state[j + 4] = ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[idx + 3] << 24);
        }
        state[14] ^= (uint32_t)cc;
        state[15] ^= (uint32_t)(cc >> 32);
        for (i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
#define QROUND(a, b, c, d)                              \
            do {                                         \
                state[a] += state[b];                    \
                state[d] ^= state[a];                    \
                state[d] = (state[d] << 16) | (state[d] >> 16);  \
                state[c] += state[d];                    \
                state[b] ^= state[c];                    \
                state[b] = (state[b] << 12) | (state[b] >> 20);  \
                state[a] += state[b];                    \
                state[d] ^= state[a];                    \
                state[d] = (state[d] << 8) | (state[d] >> 24);   \
                state[c] += state[d];                    \
                state[b] ^= state[c];                    \
                state[b] = (state[b] << 7) | (state[b] >> 25);   \
            } while (0)
            QROUND(0, 4, 8, 12);
            QROUND(1, 5, 9, 13);
            QROUND(2, 6, 10, 14);
            QROUND(3, 7, 11, 15);
            QROUND(0, 5, 10, 15);
            QROUND(1, 6, 11, 12);
            QROUND(2, 7, 8, 13);
            QROUND(3, 4, 9, 14);
#undef QROUND
        }
        for (v = 0; v < 4; v++)
            state[v] += CW[v];
        for (v = 0; v < 10; v++) {
            int byte_idx = v * 4;
            uint32_t tmp = ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 3] << 24);
            state[v + 4] += tmp;
        }
        {
            int byte_idx = 10 * 4;
            uint32_t tmp = ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 3] << 24);
            state[14] += tmp ^ (uint32_t)cc;
        }
        {
            int byte_idx = 11 * 4;
            uint32_t tmp = ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 0]) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 1] << 8) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 2] << 16) |
                           ((uint32_t)p_state[byte_idx + 3] << 24);
            state[15] += tmp ^ (uint32_t)(cc >> 32);
        }
        cc++;
        for (v = 0; v < 16; v++) {
            int base = (u << 2) + (v << 5);
            p_buf[base + 0] = (uint8_t)state[v];
            p_buf[base + 1] = (uint8_t)(state[v] >> 8);
            p_buf[base + 2] = (uint8_t)(state[v] >> 16);
            p_buf[base + 3] = (uint8_t)(state[v] >> 24);
        }
    }
    p_state[48] = (uint8_t)cc;
    p_state[49] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 8);
    p_state[50] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 16);
    p_state[51] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 24);
    p_state[52] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 32);
    p_state[53] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 40);
    p_state[54] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 48);
    p_state[55] = (uint8_t)(cc >> 56);
 
    p_ptr[0] = 0;
}
```
